# HANDOFF archive: October 2026

*History. This file carries no authority (GOVERNANCE.md, INTRO-1): it is HANDOFF.md's dated entries from October 2026, moved here unchanged on 23 Nov 2026 (governance Phase 4) so HANDOFF holds only the current version's work. A rule found only here can only be proposed; the rules are GOVERNANCE.md and its companions. Per REL-9 these entries are not edited; a wrong claim gets a dated line underneath.*

Newest first, as they stood in HANDOFF.md.

⚠️ *Corrected 23 Nov 2026 (governance Phase 4):* lines here saying "a version cut is overdue" or that a push without a version cut ships silently are out of date: since 19 Nov 2026 every changed build shows users the update bar (`tools/build-stamp.mjs`).
⚠️ *Corrected 23 Nov 2026 (governance Phase 4):* deploy notes here describe GitHub Pages only. Since v1.21.0 (18 Nov 2026) the deploy publishes to Cloudflare Pages behind the sign-in, and to GitHub Pages only for the switch-over (SEC-4, REL-3, REL-14).

### 📝 30 Oct 2026: "WHAT IF THERE WERE NO DATE SNAPPING?" — ASKED, NOT RULED, NOTHING BUILT. Tracker card `no-date-snapping`

- **The ask** (owner, *"purely hypothetically"*): drop snapping so every phase starts and ends on the exact day; the
  month view shows it, the waterfall counts it as Snap off does today. Also *"add the Dates option for default
  phases"*. ⚠️ They already have it (Weeks or Dates, ruling 7, v1.5.0). Only Production has neither, and Days is still
  custom-only (CUSTOM-PHASE-UNITS-PLAN line 41: "the ruling named Dates", not a technical reason).
- **Measured on `main` (dev server), Snap off:** a phase gets a waterfall row for every Mon–Sun week it touches by one
  day or more, numbered on: 4 wk from Wed 4/8/26 = 5 rows, `wk 1`–`wk 5`, while its meta line says `(4 wk)`. A Dates
  phase Wed 4/8 → Fri 5/1 = 4 rows; to Tue 5/5 = 5.
- ⚠️ **AN EXISTING BUG, found doing this:** Writer's Rm (Snap off, 4/8, 4 wk, ends Tue 5/5) followed by Pre-Prep (Snap
  off, starts Wed 5/6). Column placement is day-accurate (`colFreeAt <= startT`), so Pre-Prep reuses Writer's Rm's
  column, but the row is a week, so both want the cell of 5/4/26. Pre-Prep wins and `Writer's Rm wk 5` disappears from
  the waterfall. The month view is right (Wk 5 Mon–Tue, Pre-Prep Wk 1 Wed–Fri). Excel / PDF not checked; they read the
  same layout. Rare while Snap is on by default, but without snapping it would happen at every mid-week handover.
  Not fixed (computeSchedule is not frozen; the cell model is).
  - It repeats down a chain: add Prod Prep from Wed 5/27 and `Pre-Prep Wk 4` vanishes the same way. The labels then
    LOOK right (wk 1–4 for 4 weeks, Wk 1–3 for 3) only because each phase's part week was overwritten.
  - ⚠️ **"Give it its own column" is NOT a one-week column.** A phase keeps ONE column for its whole run, and must sit
    right of every phase still running at its start. The same three phases moved one day earlier (a one-day overlap,
    which is what the fix would make a handover act like) STAIRCASE: 3 columns, each phase in its own for its whole
    run. So a week-rounded `colFreeAt` widens the waterfall by a column at every mid-week handover. Fine as today's
    opt-in Snap off fix; a strong reason against "no snapping anywhere" unless the waterfall rounds each phase to
    whole weeks for itself.

### ✅ 30 Oct 2026: HOLIDAY DAYS TAKE THE WEEKEND'S GREY IN THE MONTH VIEW (FROZEN EDIT HT1) — ✅ **LIVE at `05bfdc9`** (owner's picker, *"Push now"*; `12cd4ca..05bfdc9` at 10:47:39 on 2 Nov 2026; the live `index.html` matched the gated build `3a7dcbd1…` byte for byte at 10:49:21). BUILT on branch `holiday-tint` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/holiday-tint`, from `main` at `c89ec8c`; its `node_modules` is a symlink: unlink it before `git worktree remove`), COMMITTED `6f10a6a` + `26f2065` (gate 10 and 13 baselines RE-CUT on the owner's sign-off), `main` merged in as `deaa45e`; ✅ FULL GATE PASSED 3,496 / 0 on the merged build; ✅ local `main` FAST-FORWARDED to `f3bde45` (owner, 2 Nov 2026: *"Yes, merge to main"*); PUSHED (see the head of this entry). No version cut: installed users get it on their next relaunch

- **The ask** (owner, with a screenshot of the Off hatch): *"Should holidays (not shot) days get the same background
  like this in the month view? thoughts?"* Suggested the weekend tint instead: the calendar takes a holiday off as it
  does a weekend; the hatch is the mark of a day someone took OUT of the shoot (a day-menu Off, drawn only inside the
  shoot); a worked holiday already turns orange (`.mv-day-on`). So grey = the calendar's days off, hatch = taken out,
  orange = put in. With the hatch a holiday Monday and a manual Off would look the same, and a Saturday holiday would
  sit hatched beside grey Saturdays.
- ✅ **RULED (owner, 30 Oct 2026, by picker):** *"Weekend tint"*; then *"Screen and both PDFs (Recommended)"* and
  *"Approve with that gate"* (gate: the full gate green, waterfall and Excel byte-identical, month baselines changed
  only by holiday-day fills checked line by line, sheets shown before any re-cut); then, on the sheets, *"Yes, cut
  them"*. Tracker card `holiday-shading` (it already existed from the 20 Oct gap report, item 19).
- **Which days** (`mvHolidayDays(schedule)`, beside `notesForDate`): a day is tinted only when the month view DRAWS a
  holiday's line on it: the holiday notes in `notesByIdx` through `autoNotesForView(…, 'month')` (so switched on and
  ticked for the month view), plus `monthOnlyHolidays` shown in the month view, and the line still among
  `notesForWaterfallDate()`'s lines for that day (so a both-views holiday a waterfall override dropped on purpose stays
  white). Nothing grey without its name. ⚠️ Union holidays still draw a line only inside a phase (the old rule), so a
  union holiday outside every phase stays white; the user's own holidays tint wherever the month view shows them.
- **Where it lives:**
  - `renderMonthView`: `const _holDays = mvHolidayDays(schedule)` after `_ovDays`, and `mv-holiday` pushed right
    after `mv-weekend`. A class only, so height-neutral exactly as the day marks are.
  - `legacy.css`, frozen month block: `.mv-holiday{ background:#F4F3F0 }` right after `.mv-weekend`, BEFORE the
    `.mv-day-off` / `.mv-day-on` rules, so the hatch and the worked-day tint still win by order. Soft Lines (screen
    only): the weekend rule's `#FAF9F6` now also matches `.mv-holiday`, and the spillover-white rule excludes it, so on
    screen a holiday is exactly a weekend's faint grey.
  - Month-PDF writer: `mvlParseMonth` sets `day.holiday = true` ONLY when the class is there (so a month with no
    holiday line models exactly as before; the `monthlayout` leg compares the day objects whole), and the cell fill
    is `(d.weekend || d.holiday) ? P.weekendFill`. ⚠️ Do NOT put `mv-weekend` on a holiday instead: the writer throws
    when `weekend` is not exactly Saturday/Sunday.
  - Legs taught the class: `monthlayout` (P0's expected day gains `holiday: true`) and `softlines` (a holiday counts
    with the plain weekends, `#FAF9F6`).
- **Measured before the cut** (build `cb7c30ac…`, rebuilt from `6f10a6a` to the same bytes): gate 13, 15 of 20 written
  PDFs moved, every changed line a new grey fill (`0.9569 0.9529 0.9412 rg` + `re f`) or an out-of-month holiday's fill
  and faded number recoloured (`rg` only); 5 byte-identical. Gate 10, 7 of 11 print documents moved by the class alone
  (identical once ` mv-holiday` is stripped), fit tables and sheet counts identical. Both baseline READMEs record the
  re-cut with tables.
- **Full gate** (port 8361, build `cb7c30ac…`, 30–31 Oct, load average 43–99 from other sessions' runs): 3,183 PASS,
  32 FAIL, every one a contention failure, each re-run green ALONE through the gate's own judges:

  - `onecol` 17/17, `rowmigrate` 4/4, `sideboxes` 78/78, `sbnoexport` 28/28, `colreuse` (live) 21/21,

### ✅ 30 Oct 2026: A SNAPPED START'S BOX SHOWS ITS MONDAY — ✅ **LIVE at `fdf9324`** (built on branch `snap-start-box`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/snap-start-box`, from `main` at `ea1eae6`; its `node_modules` is a symlink: unlink it before `git worktree remove`). *(Heading corrected 30 Oct 2026: it still said "not committed, not merged, not pushed" on local `main`; the branch's own HANDOFF commit `ba039cb`, which says it is live, never reached `main`.)*

- **The ask** (owner, screenshot of Pre-Prep with 03/25/2026 in the box and "Snapped to Mon 3/23/26" under the name):
  *"right now when you enter a non monday date as the start date for a phase and click enter or click out of it, it
  only shows the snapped to date in the grey text. It should also change the actual the date shows in the box.
  Otherwise this is confusing."* Tracker card `snap-start-box`.
- ⚠️ **This REVERSES one line of the `snap-monday-picker` entry below** ("Not changed, on purpose: … no field is
  rewritten"). For a TYPED value only: the picker still never rewrites a field, and switching Snap on still leaves a
  typed Wednesday (datepick P4 and P7 unchanged).
- **Built (`src/legacy/app.js`, right after the Ends box's focusout, its twin):**
  - `snapsToMonday(el)`: `start-<key>` of a real phase while `phaseSnapped(key)` (so Production with Snap on, and
    custom Weeks phases; Snap off, Days, Dates and an Ends phase's hidden box are out), plus every `.hiatus-start`
    and `.phiatus-start` (computeSchedule always snaps them). Rebuild From's `#tool-solve-date` is left out: a tool
    field, not calendar data, and not in these containers.
  - `snapTyped` (WeakSet): an `input` on a date box adds it, `focusin` clears it. `showSnappedMonday(el)` runs on
    `focusout` and on `keydown` Enter: only for a box typed in this visit, it writes `isoOf(mondayOf(d))` and fires a
    real `input` event, so every existing per-field listener (update, carryHiatusRowColour, the phase-hiatus
    delegation, DatePop's onType) runs as if the Monday had been typed. Delegated on `#phase-rows`,
    `#custom-phase-rows`, `#hiatus-list`. A bad year (parseDateUTC null) is left as typed, ringed as before.
  - **Undo:** `foldIntoBankedStep(beforeJSON)` (beside `cancelPendingUndoPush`). If the typing was already banked
    (the user paused past the 500 ms debounce), the baseline moves onto the result with no push; if the result equals
    the stack top (a Wednesday typed over its own Monday) that step is popped too. If the push is still pending it
    covers both. So one Cmd+Z takes back typing + rewrite; never a step that changes nothing.
  - Nothing reschedules (that Monday is what computeSchedule already used), so no cell, export, baseline or saved
    format moves; the saved VALUE of the field is the Monday instead of the Wednesday, which is the point. No frozen
    code, no id, no snapshot key.
- **Left alone, by design:** tabbing through a box without typing (a clean calendar stays clean), a non-Monday loaded
  from an older calendar (keeps the frozen meta's "Snapped to Mon" row until the box is edited), switching Snap to
  Monday on over a typed Wednesday (so switching it back off still gives back that day). The owner was not asked about
  the switch case; ask if it comes up.
- **Seen in the app, not changed:** the date picker opened on an EMPTY box stays on today's month while a date is
  typed (DatePop's `onType` updates the value, not the month). Pre-existing.
- **Proof:** new leg `snapbox` 12/12 (in `gate.sh` after `datepick`), red on two mutants (no rewrite: T2, U1-U3, K1,
  P1, H1; no undo fold: U1, U3). Neighbours green on the build: datepick 17/17, endsdate 17/17, snaptools 14/14,
  snapoff ×3, loadcarry 6/6, builtindates 17/17 + 15/15, customunits 23/23 + 28/28, undokeys 11/11, hiatusfold,
  hiatuscolour, hiatuslabel, phasefold, phiatusnum, calrange, numclamp, hidewf 19/19, phasesui 8/8. `npm run check`
  40/40. Seen in the browser pane with real keystrokes: Wed 3/25/26 + Enter → 03/23/2026, Thu 4/2/26 + click out →
  03/30/2026, the "Snapped to" row gone both times, one undo back past both. No full gate (sidebar behaviour only;
  nothing reaches the grid, the writers or the save format).

### ✅ 30 Oct 2026: ENDS — A WEEKS PHASE HELD BY ITS END — + THE META LINE ENDS ON THE FRIDAY — ✅ **LIVE at `d43b931`** (built as `83d2b34` on branch `weeks-end-date`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/weeks-end-date`; owner's picker, "Commit on the branch", then "Merge once main is clear", fast-forwarded as `d81655f`; the Friday line `d43b931`, fast-forwarded too; pushed at the owner's *"Ok go with choice 1, then push"*, `9b728c7..d43b931` at 17:13:49; the live `index.html` matched the tested build `2f1fe876…` byte for byte at 17:14:38). No version cut: installed users get it on their next relaunch (no service worker)

- **The ask** (owner): the idea and the snapping ruling are in the 📝 entry below; then *"build it"* after the mockup.
  Built as the mockup shows. Of the three open questions, (1) was answered after the merge (the meta line ends on the
  Friday for every Weeks phase, the owner's *"go with choice 1"*: bullet below); (2) and (3) were not, so custom
  Weeks phases get Ends too (the suggestion), and "Start after previous phase" is hidden in Ends (as the mockup
  showed). Tracker card `weeks-end-date`.
- **Where it lives (src/legacy/app.js):**
  - `phaseEnds` (beside `phaseHidden`): key -> true. Read through `phaseEndsOn(key)`, which is also false unless
    the unit is Weeks. Saved as `phaseEnds`, a sorted array, ⛔ PERMANENT, written only while non-empty
    (`phaseEndsSnapshot()` drops a flag whose phase left Weeks). Sanitized, restored unconditionally, cleared by New,
    pruned with its custom phase.
  - The END is `end-<key>` (Dates mode's box, already in fields.byId and already moved by `setPhaseStart()`); the
    visible box is the id-less `.phase-endsdate` in the new date column (`startsFieldHtml()`, built like
    `byUnitHtml()`), beside `start-<key>`, which stays in place (document order, so fields.byId's key order) and is
    hidden in Ends. The select is the id-less `select.phase-anchor`. No new id anywhere.
  - `readState()` reads the end for an Ends phase and, once both hiatus lists are read, solves its start with
    `weeksStartForEnd()` (the mirror of `computeSchedule()`'s `extendEndForHiatus()`). `computeSchedule()` is
    untouched: the state it gets is a Starts phase on that Monday.
  - ⚠️ `update()` writes that start into `start-<key>` (`syncPhaseEndsStarts()`) right after `readState()`. It is
    the ONE field `update()` writes, against `syncCustomUnitFallback()`'s rule, and it is safe only because the value
    changes only with an edit that already banks an undo step. Frozen `readCfgForMeta()` reads `start-<key>`, so
    without it the meta line would show the old start plus a bogus "Snapped to Mon" line.
  - `setPhaseStart()` moves an Ends phase's end by the same distance, rounded to whole weeks, so every mover moves
    the end. `phaseSnapped()` is true for an Ends phase (DatePop's copy reads the card's `ends-mode` class).
  - `onCustomUnitChange()` returns the phase to Starts on any unit change; leaving Ends it rewrites `end-<key>` as
    scheduled (Dates) or empties it (Days), so a later Dates switch never takes a stale end. Back to Starts
    (`setPhaseEnds()`) also empties `end-<key>`, which is what keeps a round trip byte-identical.
  - `reflectPhaseEnds()` runs in every `update()`: select value, `ends-mode` class, Ends box (left alone while
    focused; a focusout redraws it as its Friday).
  - ⚠️ The Ends box's `input` handler is a CAPTURE listener on the row containers: a custom row's own blanket
    `input -> update()` listener would otherwise run first, on the old end.
  - ⚠️ A month-view EDGE drag sets the unit to Dates with no event, so the flag lingers, unread, until the Length
    select's handler clears it. Deliberate: the drag's "back where it began" restores the unit and the flag must
    still be there.
  - CSS (legacy.css, beside the `.pu-by` rules): the column, the select drawn as `select.phase-unit`, and the Ends
    hides keyed on the card's class, never an attribute behind `:has()` (the 27 Oct lag). DatePop: kind `'friday'`.
- ✅ **THE META LINE ENDS ON THE FRIDAY, for every Weeks phase** (owner, 30 Oct 2026, after the merge: the choice was
  explained as "Friday for every Weeks phase / Friday only in Ends / leave Sunday", and they said *"Ok go with choice
  1, then push"*). Frozen `render()` still writes `start → seg.end − 1 (N wk)`, the last week row's SUNDAY;
  `reflectWeeksMetaFriday()` (beside `reflectHalfDayTotal()`) moves a Sunday end back two days. It runs from the same
  MutationObserver, which now watches `#custom-phase-rows` as well as `#phase-rows` (custom cards are a sibling).
  Idempotent: only a Sunday end moves, so its own write finds nothing to do, and a snap-off phase that starts mid-week
  (its end is already a real day, e.g. `4/8/26 → 5/19/26`) is left alone. Production (its last shoot day) and a
  Days / Dates phase (`unitmeta-<key>`) are out. Text only: no cell, export, baseline or saved byte moves. Proof:
  `endsdate` M1 (on Starts every Weeks line names a Friday, Production's untouched) and M2 (on Ends the line equals
  the box, through a month-view round trip), 17/17, both red with the rewrite disabled (they read `3/7/27`);
  `builtindates` 17/17, `customunits` 23/23, `snapoff` (sheet, friday), `loadcarry` 6/6, `phasefold` 11/11,
  `halfdays` 27/27, `datepick` 17/17, `npm run check` 40/40; seen in the browser pane on the `hidewf` fixture. No
  full gate (chrome text only; the merged gate above covers Ends).
- **Seen, not changed:** a phase with Snap to Monday OFF switched to Ends becomes whole weeks (its start moves to its
  week's Monday, its end to that week's Friday). An end inside a hiatus week cannot be worked in, so the phase ends
  the week before.
- **Proof:** new leg `endsdate` (15/15; in `gate.sh` after `datepick`), red on three mutants (no backward walk: 13
  red; movers leaving the end: S1; no Fridays-only picker: E5). `npm run check` 40/40. `customunits` and
  `builtindates` (live + restore), `datepick` 17/17, `snaptools` 14/14, `hidewf` 19/19, `phasefold` 11/11,
  `autonotephases` 17/17, `snapoff`, `sharecopy2` all green. Seen in the browser pane (the card, the Fridays-only
  picker, a pick moving the start).
  - **Full gate on build `7ef47d45…`: 3,374 PASS, 6 FAIL, every one explained.** Three were timeouts under load
    (~6.6), each green alone: `loadcarry` 6/6, `pilldrag` 30/30, `mvorder` 30/30. Three are the
    `stintswap-reshape.sptcal` fixture `62fd9ab` rewrote (below): `phasesui` P1 and `stintreshape` (9/9) pass with
    the fixture as it was at `2b15444`; gate 13's month PDF for that fixture moves because the fixture did (not
    re-run separately). The waterfall PDF matches its baseline, fields.byId has the same 77 ids, every other
    written month PDF is byte-identical, and `endsdate` is 15/15 inside the gate.
  - **`main` moved while it waited** (`4cd40b7` holiday rename, `9b728c7` the fixture restored, `88f3bb9` HANDOFF), so
    `main` was merged into the branch (only HANDOFF.md / README.md conflicted: new top entries, both kept) and the
    gate re-run on the merge `db84523`, build `979dd995…`: **3,461 PASS, 2 FAIL, both green alone** (`pilllabel`
    blocksoff, all 29 checks; `scenariocompat` on `releases/v1.2.0.html`, which this branch cannot change), at load
    ~10. `endsdate` 15/15 and `holidayrename` 14/14 on the merge. `88f3bb9` was HANDOFF only, merged after the gate.
- ✅ **FOUND, AND RESTORED ON `main` BY ANOTHER SESSION AS `9b728c7`: `tests/fixtures/stintswap-reshape.sptcal` was rewritten by `62fd9ab`**, a commit whose
  message is only "HANDOFF: Region label removal live at 9522aa3" (+379/−31 in the fixture, apparently a Save over it).
  It turns `phasesui` P1 red ("stintswap-reshape: controls / fields.byId") on `main` and on this branch alike; with
  the fixture as it was at `2b15444`, `phasesui` is 8/8. The `rebuild-from-rethink` card's gate saw the same.
- ⛔ **LESSON (this session):** with another session working in the same checkout, never stage a blob built from an
  earlier HEAD. `ff84d6f` did exactly that (a HANDOFF.md made from `3fd85ff`, staged by `git update-index` after

### 🔨 30 Oct 2026: SNAP TO MONDAY ON = THE DATE PICKER OFFERS ONLY MONDAYS — COMMITTED on `main` as `64e9a16` (owner's picker, *"Commit locally"*); ✅ **LIVE at `9b728c7`**, pushed with the holiday rename (owner's picker, *"Push now"*, 30 Oct 2026, whose question said this commit would ship with it)

- **The ask** (owner): *"when snap to monday is on wouldn't it make sense that you aren't able to pick any dates
  except mondays?"*, then *"ok do all the changes you suggested"*. Tracker card `snap-monday-picker`.
- **What was wrong:** `DatePop.jsx` decided week-vs-day by one test, `target.id !== 'custom-hol-date'`. So every
  other field got the Monday band and every day was clickable. With Snap on, a picked Wednesday sat in the Starts box
  while `computeSchedule()` started the phase that Monday. With Snap off, or on a Days/Dates phase, the band showed a
  Monday start that never happened.
- **Built:** `fieldKind(el)` in `DatePop.jsx` reads the DOM the engine reads (a local copy of `phaseSnapped()`: the
  `unit-<key>` value first, then `snap-<key>`, a missing box = snapped), and returns:
  - `'monday'`: `start-<key>` when snapped, `.hiatus-start` and `.phiatus-start` (`computeSchedule` always
    `mondayOf`s them), `#tool-solve-date` when `#tool-solve-phase`'s phase is snapped (`rebuildBackwards` pins
    `anchorMonday` then). Non-Mondays get `.dp-off` + `aria-disabled` + `tabIndex -1` and no onClick. The picked
    Monday is capped alone (`.dp-cap.dp-solo`), with no band. A hint line explains it (one wording for phases, one for
    hiatuses).
  - `'day'`: `start-<key>` when not snapped, `end-<key>`, `#custom-hol-date`, `#tool-anchor-date` while
    `#tool-anchor-edge` is `end` (`weeksToFinishBy` floors on the exact day). No band, no hint.
  - `'week'`: `#cal-range-start` / `-end` (cut by `mondayOf`) and Anchor To's start (`weeksBetweenMondays`). Unchanged.
- ⚠️ **`aria-disabled`, not `disabled`.** With `disabled`, Chrome drops mouse events on the button, so the popover's
  `onMouseDown` preventDefault never ran and a click on a greyed day took focus out of the field (seen in the browser
  pane).
- **Not changed, on purpose:** typing; the engine's own snapping; no field is rewritten (a Wednesday already in a
  snapped field stays, and the picker caps its Monday); toggling Snap leaves the date alone, so Snap off still
  restores the typed day. Old calendars open unchanged. No frozen code, no id (`.date-pop` holds none), no snapshot key.
- **Proof:** new leg `datepick` (17/17 on the build; on the old `DatePop.jsx` it goes red at P1/P2 and stops), added
  to `gate.sh` on its own line. `npm run check` 40/40, `snaptools` 14/14, `calrange` 18/18, `snapoff` and
  `builtindates` pass. Seen in the browser pane. No full gate (chrome-only rule).
- **For the Ends-mode plan below:** offering only Fridays in Ends mode is one more `fieldKind` branch returning a
  `'friday'` kind. The `'monday'` branch's disabled-day styling would carry over unchanged.

### 📝 30 Oct 2026: WEEKS PHASES COUNT BACK FROM AN END DATE — SNAPPING RULED, MOCKUP ([`mockups/weeks-end-date.html`](mockups/weeks-end-date.html)); BUILT, see the 🔨 ENDS entry above

- **The ask** (owner, 30 Oct 2026): *"what if for phases you could select EITHER starting date OR ending date. So if
  you selected ending date and then 20 weeks, it would calculate backwards"*, in Weeks mode, and whether Ends should
  have a Snap to Friday option. Tracker card `weeks-end-date`, status planned.
- **Why it is worth having:** it changes which end HOLDS. Today a phase is held by its start, so adding a hiatus or
  changing 20 weeks to 22 moves the end, often the delivery date. An Ends phase keeps the end and moves the start.
  It is not Dates mode: Dates fixes both ends and a hiatus leaves blank days, while an Ends phase still delivers its
  full week count, so a hiatus pushes its start earlier.
- ✅ **RULED (owner: "Yes, no switch"):** with Ends picked, a Weeks phase ALWAYS ends on the Friday of the week
  picked. No Snap to Friday switch; the Snap to Monday switch is hidden in Ends mode, as it already is for Days and
  Dates. The date box shows that Friday, so a typed Wednesday reads as its Friday.
  - **Why not "no snapping":** 20 × 7 days back from Fri 16 Oct 2026 starts on Sat 30 May, so the waterfall shows the
    phase in the week of 25 May too: 21 rows for a 20-week phase. Snapped it runs Mon 1 Jun to Fri 16 Oct, 20 rows.
    Rebuild From's Dates work hit the same trap (a range that began on a Saturday, an empty week row). An exact end
    day is what Dates mode is for.
- **Suggested, NOT ruled:**
  - the row's "Starts" label becomes a Starts / Ends choice over the same date box; flipping to Ends fills in the
    phase's current end, so nothing moves;
  - Production stays out (working back from a shoot-day count is a search, as `productionEndFor()` is);
  - "↳ Start after previous phase" is hidden while Ends is picked;
  - every mover moves the END, which `setPhaseStart()` (the one writer) can do in one place;
  - the calculated start keeps being written to `start-<key>`, so an older build opens the same calendar (as a
    start-held phase);
  - custom phases in Weeks get it too.
- **Hazards for whoever builds it:** Ends is a new per-phase saved setting, so a PERMANENT key or id. If it is an id
  on the static built-in rows, it needs an explicit absent → Starts default in `applyStateSnapshot()` and New (the
  trap `unit-<key>` was defaulted against), and gate 5 is re-cut. The backward hiatus walk is the mirror of
  `extendEndForHiatus()`, inside `computeSchedule()`, which is not on the frozen list. The picker should offer only
  Fridays in Ends mode, the twin of card `snap-monday-picker`.
- **The mockup** (owner: "Mock it up"): a copy of the live Post card (measured in the browser pane) with "Starts"
  as a caption select like "Length · Weeks", beside a waterfall stand-in with a "What moved" strip in the owner's
  Pushed / Pulled wording. Ends hides Snap to Monday and "Start after previous phase"; the picker (a copy of
  `DatePop.jsx` with that day's Monday-only change) offers only Fridays; a typed Wednesday or Sunday reads as its
  Friday; Dates mode drops the choice. Synthetic calendar, one live phase, a fixed hiatus. Checked in the browser
  pane with scripted and real clicks: 20 → 22 weeks in Ends pulls the start 2 wks and holds Fri 10/16/26, the
  hiatus pulls it 2 more, Starts moves the end instead; no console errors.
  - **Open for the owner:** (1) ✅ RULED 30 Oct, *"go with choice 1"*: the Friday everywhere, built (see the 🔨 Ends
    entry's "meta line" bullet). Was: the meta line under the name ends on the week's SUNDAY (frozen `render()` writes it:
    `seg.end − 1`), so it says 10/18/26 beside a box saying 10/16/26: leave it, or end it on the Friday everywhere
    (suggested; reachable without a frozen edit by the observer pattern `reflectHalfDayTotal` uses)? (2) Custom
    Weeks phases too (suggested yes)? (3) "Start after previous phase" hidden in Ends (shown), or kept and
    switching the phase back to Starts?

### 📝 29 Oct 2026: REBUILD FROM — CHOOSE WHICH PHASES IT MOVES — MOCKUP ONLY, NO APP CODE, NOTHING RULED; ➡️ LAYOUT A BUILT (entry above) ([`mockups/rebuild-from-ticks.html`](mockups/rebuild-from-ticks.html))

- **The ask** (owner, 28–29 Oct 2026): first *"should we consider getting rid of "Rebuild From""*, then *"Ok maybe we
  should keep it BUT … what if you dont want localization added by rebuild From or you do want a custom phase moved
  by rebuild from. How does it know which phases to affect?"*, then *"Mock it up"*. **Keep the tool**: it is the only
  one that works BACKWARDS from a date and the only one that fills in a phase with no date.
- **How it picks today, read from the code (`phaseSequence`, `phaseHasSpan`, `rebuildBackwards` / `rebuildForwards`),
  not reproduced in the app:**
  - The built-ins chain in `PHASE_CHAIN` order; a DATED custom phase is slotted in before the first phase that starts
    later than it; an UNDATED one goes last.
  - Every phase on the chosen side with a length is placed end to end, dated or not; one with no length is skipped.
    Nothing asks, and the popover names what moved only afterwards.
  - So Localization can be kept out only by blanking its week count. A custom phase that runs alongside (a "Casting"
    beside Prod Prep) is chained end to end and pushes Prod Prep earlier by its whole length. An undated custom phase
    is reachable only by Work forwards, after Localization.
  - Close all gaps, in the same popover, follows a DIFFERENT rule: built-ins only (`PHASE_CHAIN`), already-dated only.
- **The mockup:** the popover lists the phases on the rebuilt side in chain order, each with a tick, its new start and
  "Pushed N wks" / "Pulled N wks" / "New" / "Stays" (the owner's wording). Unticked phases stay where they are, and
  the chain carries on from the last phase placed: the existing skip rule, so the solvers barely change. One navy
  "Rebuild N phases" button replaces the two acting direction buttons.
  - **A:** pick a direction first (a segmented control), then tick.
  - **B:** one list with both sides, the pinned phase in the middle, and one button.
  - A synthetic calendar in whole weeks, without hiatuses, and an explanatory timeline under it that is NOT part of
    the design. Checked in the browser pane: both options, ticking, Rebuild, Reset; no console errors.
- **Open for the owner:** A or B. Starting ticks (shown: built-ins ticked, custom phases unticked; or also untick any
  phase that already runs alongside another). Close all gaps (leave it, or use the same ticks). Shown and not asked:
  ticks are forgotten on close, so nothing new enters the save format.
- **Building it would touch:** `PreviewToolbar.jsx` (`#pop-solve`), `runSolve` and the two solvers (a set of keys to
  skip), Anchor To's help line, and the legs that drive the tool (`snaptools`, `overrides`, `builtindates`,
  `customunits`). The ticks must carry no id, or sit inside `.tools-menu` (they do), so `collectFieldValues()` never
  sweeps them. No frozen code.

### 🔨 29 Oct 2026: THE US IS TWO PLACES, "U.S. Area Standards" AND "New York"; NEW YORK'S HOLIDAY SOURCES RE-READ — branch `us-two-places` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/us-two-places`, from `main` at `5fc7855`); FULL GATE 3,246 / 13, ALL 13 GREEN ALONE; COMMITTED `44bf663`, local `main` FAST-FORWARDED to it (owner's picker, *"Commit and merge to main"*); ✅ **LIVE at `44bf663`** (owner's picker, *"Push now"*; `ddafe45..44bf663` at 14:22:31, also carrying the docs-only `5fc7855`; the live `index.html` matched the gated build `428f5b13…` byte for byte at 14:23:22); no version cut

  - IATSE Local 52 Majors (2024–2027) **Part A § 11** (p. 49), crews in **New York and/or New Jersey**: New Year's, MLK,
    Presidents', Memorial, Fourth of July, Labor, **Veterans**, Thanksgiving, day after, Christmas, and **Juneteenth
    named, effective 1 Jan 2025**. No Good Friday. Sat → Fri, Sun → Mon, no six-day exception.
  - Local 52 **Part B § 37** (p. 84), its crews in **CT, DE, PA**: the Area Standards list (Good Friday, no Veterans).
  - **Teamsters Local 817** Feature Film Agreement (2025–2028) **Art. 11**: the same eleven as Part A, Juneteenth named;
    same weekend rule, plus a holiday on a driver's regular weekday off moving to the workday before or after (not
    modelled). Its "distant locations" include NY, NJ, CT, RI, so a Connecticut shoot can split crew and drivers.

    PROJECT-CONTEXT's "Veterans Day is AICP commercial-only, so New York only" reasoning (corrected), and "Teamsters

### ✅ 28 Oct 2026: A COLOUR SQUARE ON EVERY ALL-PHASE HIATUS CARD — built in the MAIN CHECKOUT on `main` (`e1bf969`) and COMMITTED there as `ddafe45` (owner's picker, *"Commit locally"*); ✅ **LIVE at `ddafe45`** (owner's picker, *"Push now"*, over the recommended "Not yet"; `cc3ea13..ddafe45` at 22:35:32, also carrying the docs-only `e1bf969`; the live `index.html` matched the tested build `c5906f52…` byte for byte at 22:36:23); no full gate

- **Asked** (owner): *"add the color picker menu to the all phase hiatuses in the left side bar just like you can pick
  color for phases"*. Tracker card `hiatus-card-colour`.
- **Built** (`src/legacy/app.js`, block "AN ALL-PHASE HIATUS'S COLOUR, FROM ITS CARD" after `removeHiatusRow`; no frozen
  code, no saved key, no id):
  - `addHiatusRow()`: a `.swatch.hiatus-swatch` between the caret and the name. CSS (`legacy.css`, after `.hiatus-head`)
    puts it in the head's flow at exactly the phase square's place (36 px in, 12 down, name at 58), and the head's tint
    now reads `--hiatus-color` (falling back to `--c-hiatus`), written on the row like a phase card's `--phase-color`.
  - A click runs `openHiatusColourPop()`: the phase picker (`openPhaseColorPop`) with `EXCEL_STANDARD_COLORS`, the band
    palette the waterfall band editor and the month day menu's *Colour…* already use. A pick runs
    `setHiatusRowColour()` (writes `hiatusColors` for EVERY week; red deletes the keys) then `update()`, one undo step.
    The day menu's *Colour…* (`hiatusColourHere`) now calls the same two, and its private `bandSpan` is the shared
    `hiatusRowSpan()`, so there is one copy of the span rule.
  - `reflectHiatusSwatches()` paints the square and the tint from the hiatus's first week OF ITS OWN (one no other
    all-phase hiatus covers), from `update()` and from a `#table-wrap` childList observer, because the band editor and
    the day menu redraw with `render()` alone.
  - ⭐ **Decided, not asked: the colour follows a retyped Starts or Weeks** (`carryHiatusRowColour`, run on the row's
    own `input` before `update()`, against `row._colourSpan`, the span the last paint saw; a blank field keeps the last
    real span). Without it a colour picked on the card stayed on the old weeks while the band moved, which is the
    known gap the hiatus drag entry (19 Oct) recorded. A hiatus whose own weeks share one colour carries it to every
    new week; a mixed one moves week for week and an added week takes the last week's (the drag's rule 4). A week
    another all-phase hiatus covers is never written, on or off, so a hiatus moved across another doesn't recolour it
    and comes back whole. ⚠️ Only the COLOUR follows: a band's typed label, size and text style still stay on the old
    weeks when the dates are retyped (unchanged; the drag carries them, typing doesn't).
  - Help: one sentence in the All-phase hiatus paragraph.
- **Save format: unchanged.** `hiatusColors` was always in `captureSnapshot()`, sanitized as hex and read by every
  renderer and writer. `fields.byId` and a hiatus row's saved keys are unchanged (leg C9).
- **Verified** (build of the working tree, `npm run check` 40/40):
  - New leg **`hiatuscolour` 11/11** (`tests/harness/t/hiatuscolour.js`; in `gate.sh` on its own line after
    `hiatusfold`). Red on a `git archive` build of `main` (9 of 11: everything but C9 and E0) and on a mutant with the
    carry removed (C5–C8).
  - Targeted legs on the build: `hiatusfold` 11/11, `mvmenu (halfdays)` 81/81 (the day menu's *Colour…* now runs
    through the shared helpers), `wfmenu (halfdays)` 39/39 (its Remove hiatus… reads `bandSpan`), `phasecolors`
    10/10, `hiatuslabel` 7/7, `hiatusblank` 4/4, `loadcarry` 6/6, `hiatusdrag` 14/14 (it first hit its 480 s
    deadline with no case run, in a batch on port 8262; 14/14 alone in 2 min). `phasesui (hidewf)` 7/8: P1 fails on
    `stintswap-reshape` only, and fails IDENTICALLY on the `main` build without this change. ⚠️ Cause: the main
    checkout's `tests/fixtures/stintswap-reshape.sptcal` carries ANOTHER session's uncommitted edit (+378/−30, in
    `git status` before this work began), so it no longer matches `tests/baselines/2026-10-23-phasesui/before.json`.
    Not this change; left untouched, and it will turn P1 red in any gate run from this checkout until it is resolved.
  - In the browser pane on `hiatusdrag.sptcal`: the square sits where a phase card's does, a fixture band recoloured in
    the waterfall shows on its card, a pick colours all three Summer Hiatus weeks and the head, Weeks 3 → 4 adds a
    coloured week, a new Starts moves the colour.

### ✅ 28 Oct 2026: DOUBLE-CLICK A MONTH BAR TO OPEN ITS PHASE'S CARD — ✅ LIVE at `7965ebc` — BUILT in the main checkout and COMMITTED on `main` on top of `c2a2949`, the month stepper's merge (owner's picker, *"Commit locally"*); no full gate; PUSH APPROVED together with the month stepper (owner's picker, *"Push both now"*)

- **Asked** (owner): *"When you double click on a phase in the month view, could it reveal that phase menu in the
  left side bar"*. Tracker card `mv-dblclick-phase-card`.
- **Built** (app.js, beside `showPhaseRow()` in `installMonthDayMenu`): a document-level `dblclick` listener, month
  view only, that runs the day menu's own **Show in Phases card** (⬆️ since the menu trim merged in, 28 Oct, no longer
  in the menu: the double-click is its only door) (`showPhaseRow(key)`: the Phases tab, the card
  unfolded, scrolled to centre, one undo step because `sidebarTab` is saved state). Prep's bars open the Prep card.
  The Sim Post bar (no `data-ph`) does nothing, as the menu offers it nothing. Help: one clause in "What sits on top
  in the Month view". No frozen code, no saved key, no id.
- ⭐ **Why it is on the DOCUMENT and finds the bar BY POINT** (`mvTextAt()`, the Text bar's pick): the edge handles
  and the six-dot grip lie over the bar's ends, and a press on a handle appends `.phase-edge-shield`. The release
  lands on the shield, so Chrome sends the click and the dblclick to the common ancestor, `<body>`.
  `e.target.closest('.mv-pill')` on `#table-wrap` would never hear a double-click near a bar's end. The mutant
  that does exactly that fails leg case B3 only.
  - Guards: a second press that travelled past `MV_SEL_SLOP` (the end of a drag), a live drag shield, a Mac Ctrl+click.
- ⚠️ **Learned (harness): a synthetic double-click on an edge handle needs a pointer MOVE before each press.** The
  first press hides the pooled handles (`hideHandles`). With no mousemove between the clicks, the second press lands
  on the bar itself, and a test of the shield path silently tests the plain path instead (the `e.target` mutant
  passed until the move was added). A real mouse always moves.
  - Also: a shield that comes and goes inside one dispatch is invisible to a `MutationObserver` that queries the DOM
    in its callback. Read the records' `addedNodes`.
- **Proof:** new leg `t/mvbarcard.js` (gate.sh AFSPEC, own line after `hiatusfold`) 7/7 on the build. Red on the build
  without the listener (B1–B4) and with the bar found by `e.target` (B3). Neighbours green on the same build:
  `mvtextstyle` 23 + `(mvtextstyle)` 6, `mvmenu (halfdays)` 89, `halfworked (dayoverrides-onhalf)` 24,
  `phasefold` 11, `mvnotes` 30, `mvbarsize (mvnotes)` 13, `monthmap (mvnotes)` 20; `npm run check` 40/40. In the
  pane with a real mouse at 1280 × 800: Set Build's bar and Shoot Ep. 101 each opened their card on screen, with
  dates unchanged. (This pane's tab pill lagged a screenshot behind the page again. Measure, don't eyeball.)
- ⚠️ **The pane's five-server limit for this folder was full** (other chats), so the check used the main checkout's
  existing harness server on 8390. A `mvbarcard-8619` entry was added to `.claude/launch.json`, unused.
- **No full gate:** another session's gate (`ep-shoot-bars`) was running, and the change has the hiatus-fold
  profile: no frozen code, no saved byte, no id.
- ✅ **Pushed and live:** ONE `git push origin main` at 12:02:02 (`9d5fe4a..7965ebc`). It also carried the month
  stepper (`2ee0a21`, `f151902`, `c2a2949`) and the HANDOFF-only `646ff9d`: the owner chose *"Push both now"*,
  asked with the stepper named. First a rebuild from the commit was `cmp`-identical to the tested build
  `d3042c39…`. At 12:03:11 the live `index.html`, fetched with a cache-buster, was byte-identical to it (1,875,118
  bytes). No version cut (still v1.9.0).
- ⏭ **Next:** the next full gate includes `mvbarcard`, and the month stepper's merged build, which had no full gate.

### ✅ 28 Oct 2026: ALL-PHASE HIATUS CARDS FOLD TO THEIR HEAD, LIKE PHASE CARDS — ✅ LIVE at `9d5fe4a` — branch `hiatus-fold` (worktree `.claude/worktrees/hiatus-fold`), cut from `main` at `73de2b6`; COMMITTED as `5575e89`, merged with `main` (`c6cdfd3`) and local `main` FAST-FORWARDED to it (owner's picker, *"Commit and merge to main"*); no full gate (owner's question, answered below); ✅ PUSHED (owner's picker, *"Push now"*)

- **Asked** (owner): *"Lets give collapsing arrows to the all phase hiatuses just like phases"*. Tracker card
  `hiatus-card-fold`.
- **Built** (app.js "THE CARET ON EVERY ALL-PHASE HIATUS CARD", beside `removeHiatusRow`; legacy.css "Phase card
  caret"): `addHiatusRow()` puts a `.hiatus-collapse-btn` (a `<button>` with NO id) first in `.hiatus-head`; it shares
  the phase caret's CSS. Folded, `.hiatus-body` hides and the card keeps the folded phase card's 4 px white edge.
  Session UI, exactly the phase fold's rules: not in `captureSnapshot()`, never an undo step. Show in Phases card
  (`showHiatusRow`) unfolds its card. ⬆️ *(Since the menu trim merged in, 28 Oct, that item and `showHiatusRow` are
  gone: ruling T1. `hiatusfold` H8 checks the menu offers none.)*
- ⭐ **The one thing that differs from the phase fold: a hiatus row has NO KEY** (`fields.hiatuses` saves rows by
  position), so there is no `collapsedPhaseKeys` twin. The fold lives on the row as `.is-collapsed`, and every
  rebuild carries it: `applyStateSnapshot()` step 2 reads `hiatusFolds()` before emptying `#hiatus-list` and calls
  `carryHiatusFolds()` after. Same row count → position for position. Different count (an undone ×, a redone Add) →
  each folded row is found again by its own values (`hiatusRowSig`: start, weeks, name, lock, numbered).
  `probeScenarioTabs` and `applyStateSnapshotAtomically`'s rollback save and put back the folds the same way. The two
  Load / recovery sites call `unfoldAllHiatuses()` beside `collapsedPhaseKeys.clear()`, AFTER the apply (which has
  already carried the old calendar's folds in). New needs nothing: `addDefaultHiatuses()` rebuilds open rows.
- **Proof:** new leg `t/hiatusfold.js` (gate.sh AFSPEC, beside `phasefold`) 11/11 on the build. Red on `main` before
  it (`dist-pre`: 9 of 11; H3 and E0 green, as they should be) and on a mutant with the carry removed (H5–H9).
  `npm run check` 40/40. Neighbours, all green on the build: `phasefold` 11, `sharecopy2`, `hiatusblank` 4,
  `hiatuslabel` 7, `spanhint` 9, `badyear` 6, `loadcarry` 6, `dlgkeys` 16, `mvmenu (halfdays)` 89,
  `wfmenu (halfdays)` 38, `labelsetup (halfdays)` 41, `phiatusnum` 18, `phasesui (hidewf)` 8. Those ran on the build
  before the folded card's 4 px edge was added (one rule, folded state only); on the final build `b275b591…`, check
  40/40 and `hiatusfold` 11/11 again (folded card 61 px).
  Seen in the pane: folded cards show the red head, caret pointing right; open ones are unchanged.

### 🔨 27 Oct 2026: TWO OR MORE SEASONS TICKED IN SHOW INFO'S SEASON DROPDOWN, NAMED IN THE HEADER. Built in the main checkout, COMMITTED on `main` as `15ac6ce` (owner's picker, by the month-bar-size session), merged for the single v1.9.0 gate (entry above); ✅ LIVE in v1.9.0 at `9282356`

- **Asked** (owner): *"what do we do if multiple seasons (usually max of 2) are shot simultaenously and would be on
  the same calendar"*. After an answer about one crew or two, they narrowed it: *"no its really just about being able
  to select two or more seasons in the Show info input and having those seasons translate to the header"*. Tracker
  card `two-seasons-one-calendar`. **Picked:** "Tick any seasons" (not a "Season … to …" range), "S3 & S4" (three:
  "S3, S4 & S5"). First built as ten ticks in the card; then the owner: *"I'd like to keep the selector as a drop down
  menu with the checkboxes inside the drop down"*, so it is a dropdown again.
- **Built** (no new id, no `SNAPSHOT_VERSION` bump):
  - `SeasonPop.jsx` (new, mounted in `#react-root` beside `SelectPop`): `#season-num` stays the visible native
    NativeSelect; a mousedown on it is preventDefault'd (Chrome's list never opens) and a fixed panel opens below it,
    `.select-pop.season-pop`, ten `<button data-season>` rows with a drawn box (no id, no form field). Keys that
    would change a native select's value (arrows, Home/End, typeahead, Enter/Space) open the panel instead, so a
    keystroke can never quietly drop a second season. While open: arrows move, Space/Enter tick via the row's
    click, Escape closes (consumed), Tab closes, an outside press closes, it follows scroll.
  - Bridge `chrome.seasons(list)` (pushed by `reflectSeasons()` in `update()`) feeds a tiny store (`useSeasons`) read
    by the panel AND by `SeasonField` in `Sidebar.jsx`, which relabels the first season's option "Seasons 3 & 4"
    (text only, never a value). The engine takes a row's `click` on document by `data-season`. ⛔ Click, not a
    checkbox `change`: a controlled checkbox would be restored by React inside the click, before the engine reads it.
  - `app.js`: `seasonList`, `seasonsShown()`, `seasonLabel()`, `reflectSeasons()` (called from `update()`), sitting
    beside `episodeNumberFor`. `#season-num` holds the FIRST ticked season, so episode numbering and older builds keep
    working. ⛔ New PERMANENT key `seasons` (ascending ints 1–10), written only with two or more. Sanitized: bad entries
    dropped and counted, and a list left with one is dropped whole. Restored unconditionally after the field replay;
    a list that disagrees with `season-num` wins and sets it. A change event on `#season-num` itself means ONE season,
    which keeps every `T.set('season-num', …)` leg working.
  - The three header readers (`computeHeaderDefaults`, `computeMvHeaderDefaults`, `buildHeaderCtx`) use
    `seasonLabel()`. None is frozen. One season reads `'S' + n`, exactly as before.
- ⛔ **FROZEN EDIT A1, `exportExcel`** (owner's picker, 27 Oct 2026: *"Yes, apply it (Recommended)"*):
  - `hdrSafe` writes `&` as `&&` instead of deleting it. Before, "S3 & S4" printed in Excel as "S3  S4".
  - Second half, disclosed when it was added: the 255-character shave never leaves a lone `&` before the `…`.
  - `estimateExcelHeaderLength()` counts the doubled `&` too.
  - Gate: the Excel baselines are byte-identical (base.xlsx's header has no `&`, checked), plus leg S4/S4b.
- **Proof:** new leg `t/seasons.js` (in `gate.sh` AFSPEC, 400 s), **15/15** on the build, the dropdown driven by mouse
  (hit-tested) and keyboard. `npm run check` 40/40. Checked by eye in the pane: the closed field reads "Seasons 3 & 4",
  the open list shows ten rows with boxes, 3 and 4 ticked. Its own full gate was stopped by the owner's call;
  ✅ **v1.9.0's single release gate** (with the three other features): 3,338 PASS, 0 FAIL, `seasons` 15/15 in it.
- ⚠️ **Harness trap found:** after a Load through `T.openViaFakePicker`, `#file-menu` is closed
  (`aria-expanded="false"`) but its dropdown stays drawn (display block, 153,50 280×293), because its closing transition

  `onTop()` tolerates exactly that case.

    red, then green. It now carries the month band style (`mvTextStyle.band`) too.
  - **M2 + gate 10 re-cut, all eleven cases** (only the note tooltips moved; baseline README has the table).
  - **Sizes measured (plan §3.3a): bars and bands 6–11**, not 12 (12 cuts descenders by 1 px on screen), notes 6–10,
    month name 6–17, weekday row 6–11. Held line boxes moved no row.
  - Store `mvTextStyle` / save key `mvTextStyles` / `fmt` on a day note (CLAUDE.md state table); the month highlight
    and R1; M1; the writer W1–W5.
- **Decided while building (plan §12):** prep bars keyed by unit, block bars by block id, un-numbered Sim Post by its
  would-be N; bold toggles 600 → 400 → 700 on a bar (header rule); the month name's alignment control greys; the
  outline is white-in-purple (a default note is the highlight's purple); `.month-sel-box` stripped from shareable copies.
- ⚠️ **Learned:**
  - M1's `line-height:15px` on a sized bar is INHERITED by the grey `.mv-pill-block` as a length, moving its baseline up
    to 1 px; the writer copies that. `monthemit` found it.
  - `monthemit` compared starts, which is wrong for aligned text (Chrome kerns, the writer does not). It now anchors
    each text at its alignment's anchor, and a centred/right note line at its VISIBLE characters (`pre-wrap`'s hanging
    space).
  - The highlight resolver must not call writer names (`mvlDecls`): the month legs' F0 forbids it.
  - A `t/` leg's own source is injected into the page, so a shareable copy contains it: never search a copy for a
    literal the leg also contains.
- **Verified:** `mvtextstyle` LIVE 23/23, RESTORE 6/6, v1.8.0 opens the file; `monthemit` 16/16 on the styled fixture;
  `hiatusdrag` 14/14; `textstyle`, `mvnotes` green; `npm run check` 40/40. **Mutants (plan §8): all twelve red, each
  on its own case** (listed in `gate.sh` above the mvtextstyle block). ⚠️ `monthemit` slices `/src/legacy/app.js`
  unless given `HARNESS_QUERY=src=<path>`, so a mutant BUILD alone leaves it testing the real writer (it went green
  until run with `src=`). And an undo re-sanitizes its snapshot, so a bad value written by the bar can vanish before a
  later Save sees it: check by Save straight after the press.
- **Full gate, run 1** (`8f5ec75`, 13:21–14:23, 62 min): **7 FAIL**, fixed in `c9514cf`:
  - ⛔ **R1 broke three older cases, which expected a click on a month note to open its editor:** `mvmenu` L4 (a
    synthetic press-release-click), `mvorder` N5 and N6 (trusted CDP input; N6 THREW, `.focus()` on an editor that
    never opened, so the leg looked like a crash, not a rule change). Each now holds R1: a press picks (outline drawn,
    no editor, no menu, no drag), then a double-click opens. ⚠️ `el.click()` from a script (no press) still opens a
    note, which is why `mvnotes` and `mvmenu` P7 never noticed. Any new leg that opens a month note by a real click
    must double-click.
  - `monthlayout` EX on all four fixtures: it slices the writer out of app.js, anchored on `buildMonthLayout`'s last
    line, which W3 changed. Re-anchored, and it hit two things the slice lacked. (1) The model read `MVL_PAINT`, the
    emitter's constant declared AFTER it. `mvlStyledWidthOf` now reads it only once a style needs it, which only the
    export does (bytes unchanged). (2) M1's `MV_TEXT_LINE_PX`, now sliced in too. F0's names gain `mvlTextFmt`,
    `mvlFrameFmt` and `mvlStyledWidthOf`.
  - `monthexport (hiatus-blanklabel)` wrote no file, so gate 13 had none for it: a stall. Standalone 9/9, and its file
    is byte-identical to gate 13's baseline. The other 21 gate 13 files were byte-identical in the run.
- ✅ **Full gate, run 2** (`c9514cf`, 14:57–15:41, no other gate running at its start): **3,096 PASS, 3 FAIL, all three
  stalls.** `papersize` and `undokeys` produced no result and `pdfdialog` stopped after D2. These are waterfall and undo
  legs this branch does not touch, and all three passed in run 1. Standalone on port 9905 against this build:
  papersize 12/12, pdfdialog 15/15, undokeys 11/11. Every run-1 failure is green: mvmenu L4, monthlayout 56/56, mvorder
  30/30, monthexport (hiatus-blanklabel). Gate 13: 22/22 byte-identical. Gate 10 (`monthprint`): 67/67 against the
  re-cut.
  ⚠️ Running a leg beside another session's gate: `run.sh` and the `.mjs` drivers `pkill` the `srv.js` on THEIR port
  before starting their own. So a standalone run on the default 8231 kills a running gate's server. Use a
  non-default `HARNESS_PORT`.

### ✅ 27 Oct 2026: AUTOMATIC NOTES FOR EACH PHASE'S START AND END, OFF BY DEFAULT. ✅ LIVE at `273692c` (pushed alone on top of `31eddd0`; owner's picker: "Commit and merge", "Push it alone, once verified")

- **Asked** (owner): *"what are all the other automatic notes we could have?"*, then *"do the phase starts and ends.
  they should be disabled by default."* Tracker card `more-auto-note-kinds`. The other ideas offered (Production
  resumes after a break, block starts, halfway, Sim Post begins, opt-in notes for custom phases) were NOT asked for.
- **What it is:** nine kinds beside ruling 9's three, `AUTO_NOTE_PHASE_EDGES` in `app.js`: `writersRoomEnd`
  ("Writer's Room Closes"), and `prePrep` / `prodPrep` / `post` / `localization` + `Start` / `End` ("<phase name>
  Begins|Ends"). ⛔ The kind words are SAVE FORMAT (the key's and `data-autonote`'s).
- ⭐ **Why a second store.** `autoNotesOff` means "absent = shown", so a new kind put there would have appeared on
  every calendar ever saved, widening its Notes column and its exports. The new kinds live in **`autoNotesOn`**
  ("absent = hidden"): a new snapshot key written only while one is ticked, sanitized like `autoNotesOff` (only
  `true`, unknown words kept), restored unconditionally, cleared by `resetAll()`. `autoNoteKindShown(kind)` is the one
  question both stores answer, asked by `autoNotesForView()` and by the card's boxes. No `SNAPSHOT_VERSION` bump: an
  older build ignores the key and simply shows none of them.
- **Dates:** computeSchedule dates each note on the first or last day the month view DRAWS the phase's bar
  (`segCoversDate`, outside every hiatus and the phase's own), so the note and the bar cannot disagree: a week-based
  phase ends on its last Friday, and a phase paused at its start begins after the pause. Writer's Room Opens still
  uses the raw `wrSeg.start`, unchanged on purpose (it would move existing notes). The new notes are added in date
  order AFTER the old ones, so no older note moves within its week. Tagged with `phase`, so Hide in waterfall
  (PHASES-TAB-PLAN R4) takes them out of the waterfall only. Built-ins only: custom phases are still never auto-noted.
- **Names:** the four phases' notes follow a name typed in the Phases tab ("Edit Begins"), as their bars do;
  `reflectAutoNoteKinds()` writes that name into the card row's `data-autonote-name` span. Writer's Room and
  Production keep their own words whatever the phase is called (as "Writer's Room Opens" always has).
- **Card** (`AutoNotesCard`, Sidebar.jsx): one row per phase, its name, then a start box and an end box (`.autonote-list`
  is a 3-column grid now). Still plain checkboxes with NO id. ⚠️ **Measured:** the card is ~245 px wide in a narrow
  window, and a 12 px column gap cut "Writer's Room" to "Writer's Ro…"; it is 8 px, and a longer typed name wraps.
  A Help sentence (src/index.html, the notes paragraph) points at the card.
- **No frozen code changed.** computeSchedule and `autoNotesForView` are not on the list; the notes reach the
  writers as data, exactly as the three old kinds do. With nothing ticked, every output is what it was.
- **Proof:** new leg `tests/harness/t/autonotephases.js` (AFSPEC, 400 s): 17/17 on the build, **15 of 17 red** on the
  pre-change build (`dist-pre/`, main at `51ad3fd`, `9df703dd…`; the two green are the month-PDF control and E0). It
  checks the default, the card, all nine in every output, the month view's day, undo, rename, a phase hiatus, Hide in
  waterfall, Save/Load + the M-5 trap, a REAL `v1.8.0-saved.sptcal` opening with none of them, and New.
  `autonotekinds` 16/16 (A1 now reads each box's row name + word: "Production Starts"). `npm run check` 40/40.
  **Full gate** on build `0ba127aa…` (12:19 onward, after the main checkout's own gate finished): **3,101 PASS, 1
  FAIL**, the known contention flake `pilllabel (shootorder)` W1 ("months 1 vs 18"), **29/29** re-run alone through
  gate.sh's own judge. Every baseline byte-identical (waterfall PDF, Excel parts, every gate-13 month PDF), 0 clipped.
- **Shipped:** committed on `autonote-phases` as `db0dc69`, then cherry-picked alone onto live `31eddd0` (branch
  `autonote-phases-live`, worktree `.claude/worktrees/autonote-live`) as `273692c`, so main's other unpushed commits
  (plans, month bar size step 1) did not ship with it. On that build (`7ffc4f63…`): `autonotephases` 17/17,
  `autonotekinds` 16/16, `settingswin` 21/21, `npm run check` 40/40. Pushed `31eddd0..273692c`; the live page served
  the new build (it carries `writersRoomEnd`). Merged into `main` afterwards; the month-bar-size session's
  uncommitted HANDOFF entry in the main checkout was set aside for the merge (backup + patch, no stash) and put back.

### ✅ 27 Oct 2026: MONTH -> WATERFALL SWITCH ABOUT 4x FASTER (one sidebar selector + frozen edit L1, owner sign-off by picker). ✅ LIVE at `31eddd0` (owner's picker: "Commit", "Only this fix"; the live page served the gated build `46a97f5c` byte for byte ~46 s later)

- **Asked** (owner, after `ae39e3e` went live): *"I think the lag when you switch from month view to waterfall is
  still there"*. Tracker card `view-switch-lag`.
- **What the 26 Oct fix left:** it only removed what `71a6c87` added. Every WATERFALL redraw (switch, or any
  `update()` in the waterfall) cost ~105 ms in the pane, ~60–70 ms headless; month redraws ~12 ms.
- **Cause, profiled over CDP** (scratch scripts, not in the repo): ~51 of ~67 ms in frozen `installGridResizers`.
  It appended a handle, then read a row's rect, once per row in two loops, so ~100 forced style recalcs.
  Each recalc restyled ~105 elements because of ONE rule: `.phase-row:has(select.phase-unit …) [id^="meta-"]`.
  Found by deleting `:has()` rules over CSSOM against a 100-insert benchmark: all off 12 ms, that rule alone +23.
- **Fix 1** (`legacy.css`): `[id^="meta-"]` → `.phase-meta[id^="meta-"]`, same elements, with a ⛔ comment.
  Halved the switch on its own (60–71 → 29–37 ms).
- **Fix 2, ⛔ FROZEN EDIT L1** (owner's picker, 27 Oct 2026: *"Yes, apply it (Recommended)"*): `installGridResizers`
  reads `rowBoxes` once, after the column handles (where the first row read always was). Both row loops use it.
  - **Acceptance gate:** every `.grid-resize` handle's class, inline style and dataset identical to the pre-change
    build `9df703dd`. Checked on mvnotes, customunits, builtindates, colreuse, hidewf, carry-rich,
    custom-nondense, notewrap, stintswap-chained, xlsx-overlimit and month-dense60, at zoom 1, 1.5 and 0.75:
    all identical. Phase hint visibility identical too. Plus the full gate.
- **Measured** (synchronous click, medians, headless): 52 weeks 59–67 → 13–15 ms at 1440 and 742 px; a longer show
  81 → 22 ms. Waterfall → month is ~14 ms, so both directions now match.
- ⚠️ **Lessons:**
  - Behind a `:has()`, never use an attribute-only subject (`[id^=…]`). Key it on a class.
  - Chrome re-checks `:has()` rules on EVERY insertion here, presumably because a `:has()` argument holds a
    `:not()` (`#prep-rows:not(:empty)`) or a structural pseudo (Mantine's `:only-child`). So any frozen loop that
    interleaves appends and rect reads pays every `:has()` rule per row.
  - Unwrapping lightningcss's `:is(...)` merges saved only ~3 ms.
  - Measure in headless Chrome over CDP (`Profiler`, `Tracing`). The desktop pane's timings swing with its
    width and visibility.

  (they rescan the body). With L1 there is one recalc per redraw, so they no longer add up here. A body class set
  beside `.active` in `setViewMode()` and `applyStateSnapshot()` would remove them if another loop ever needs it.

### 📝 27 Oct 2026: STYLE ANY TEXT IN THE MONTH VIEW WITH THE TEXT STYLE BAR: PLANNED, EVERYTHING RULED, M1 + M2 SIGNED OFF ([`MONTH-TEXT-STYLE-PLAN.md`](MONTH-TEXT-STYLE-PLAN.md); superseded by the BUILT entry at the top)

- ✅ **Ruled by picker, 27 Oct 2026** (plan §2, §6):
  - **N1, in the owner's own words: *"maybe disable recoloring phase bar text"*.** Read as: no text colour on any bar
    drawn as a pill (phase, episode, Simultaneous Post), since all three ring themselves in their text colour. Bands,
    notes, the month name and the weekday row keep colour. The bar's colour control greys while only bars are picked,
    and the sanitizer drops a bar's colour. So M1 writes no ring and the writer reads no `border-color`. "maybe" = the
    owner may revisit it; the format would not change.
  - **N2: "Follows Prep wk 2 (Recommended)".** **N3: "Keeps its own look (Recommended)".**
  - **Sign-off: "Yes, M1 and M2 as planned (Recommended)"**, with plan §6's gate (gate 10 re-cut for M2's tooltips only,
    then 10 and 13 byte-identical unstyled; 0 clipped glyphs, rows and PDF scale unchanged on a styled fixture; the
    waterfall's exports untouched).
- **Next:** (done; see the entry above.)

- **The plan is written** (owner: *"Write MONTH-TEXT-STYLE-PLAN.md from the four rulings, with the frozen edit and its
  gate spelled out for sign-off"*, adding *"actually no dont let the day number be editable"*, which confirms R4).
  Uncommitted. It supersedes the scoping bullets below where they differ:
  - **Store:** a separate key, `mvTextStyles`, NOT new kinds in `textStyles` (R2: the waterfall's code iterates
    `TEXT_STYLE_KINDS`). Phase bars are keyed `<phase>@<its week number>`, so a style follows "Prep wk 2" through
    every mover with no re-key code (N2). Episodes by id; bands by the `hiatusTexts` keys; a month note's style is `fmt`
    on its own `dayNotes` entry.
  - **Every style keeps its text's line box**, so only a note's line count can move (a bold note may take one more line).
  - ⚠️ **TWO frozen edits, not one.** M1 (styles appended, inert unstyled) and **M2: the notes' tooltips** "click to
    edit" -> "double-click to edit", which R1 makes necessary. Gate 10's baselines hold those `title` attributes
    (checked: 4–17 per case), so M2 needs gate 10 re-cut, with a diff showing only titles changed.
  - N1–N3 were open here; ruled the same day (above).
- ⚠️ **Found while planning, a bug in shipped code (read from the code, not reproduced):** the month view's hiatus drag
  (`installHiatusDrag` `rekey()`/`setMaps()`) carries a band's text, name sync, colour and font size, but NOT
  `textStyle.hiatus`. So a band styled from the bar in the waterfall and then dragged in the month leaves its style
  behind. `shiftCalendar` does carry it. It is a one-map fix, not frozen code. Plan step 0.

- **Asked** (owner): *"What would it take to allow styling any text in the month view calendar with the styling
  menu"*. Tracker card `month-text-style`, requested. It covers the bold/colour half of `note-styles`, and its size
  question overlaps `month-bar-size` (the entry below).
- **Today:** in the month view the bar styles the month header lines only (TEXT-STYLE-BAR-PLAN D4). `renderMonthView`
  never calls `textStyleOf()`. The month PDF writer reads only `background` and `color` from a bar's inline style
  (`mvlParseItem`) and draws every bar at `MVL_PAINT.bar` (10 px 600) and every note at 500. A single click on a
  month note opens its editor, and press-and-move drags it.
- **What it would take** (the waterfall bar's shape, one writer fewer: the month has no Excel):
  - A highlight in the month view (bars and notes are elements, so it can outline their rects), plus D5's rule there:
    click picks, double-click edits a note. That changes a month habit, so it needs a ruling.
  - Store: new kinds in `textStyles`. Keys: date (day numbers), `week|phase` (pills, bands), episode id, week (Sim Post,
    all-phase band). A month note's style goes on its own `dayNotes` entry, so it travels with drag, shift and paste.
    ⚠️ Older builds: `cleanTextStyles` drops unknown kinds (bad.n, console warning only, `lastSanitizeDropped` is not
    shown), and the `dayNotes` sanitizer copies unknown entry fields through (`Object.assign`). So no `SNAPSHOT_VERSION` bump.
  - Frozen edit to `renderMonthView`: append each text's style to its inline style (F1's shape). With no style it is
    inert, so gates 10 and 13 stay byte-identical, and the print rollback (`exportMonthPdf`) follows because it prints
    that DOM. "Day N" is a `::after` and cannot take an inline style: it would need a frozen CSS edit (custom properties).
  - The writer (not frozen; gate 13 byte for byte): weight, italic, colour and alignment per item. Inter 400/500/600/700
    are all embedded already; the italic slant exists (`MVL_PAINT.italicSkew`, used by `mvlLineStyle` for header lines).
  - Note measurement: `mvNoteLineCount` (screen, 10 px 500) and `mvlNoteLines` (writer) must measure in the note's own
    style, or a bold note loses its last line (the waterfall's F4 problem).
- **Size is the hard part again.** Screen lanes are a fixed 17 px. A 10 px note line (13 px) exactly fills a lane's
  padding box, so bigger note text needs more lanes (rows grow, and dense months shrink in the PDF). Bar text has room up
  to about 12 px with a capped line-height, by arithmetic: not measured.
- ✅ **RULED, owner's picker, 27 Oct 2026** (every recommendation taken, except that day numbers are out):
  - Click: **"Click picks, double-click edits (Recommended)"**. D5's rule in the month view: a click picks a note,
    and a double-click, Enter or typing opens its editor. Dragging a note still moves it.
  - Shared?: **"Separate per view (Recommended)"**. Month styles never touch the waterfall's and the reverse, including a
    waterfall note shown in the month. So the month gets its own kinds, never `textStyle.note` / `.phase`.
  - Size: **"Only sizes that fit (Recommended)"**. Bars up to about 12 px, notes 10 px or smaller. The calendar never
    changes shape and month PDFs print at the same scale. Bigger text stays with `month-bar-size`.
  - Which text: **"Bars and bands", "Notes", "Month name and weekday row"**. **Day numbers were NOT picked**, so they
    are out. That also takes "Day N" out, and with it the frozen CSS edit it needed.
- **What follows from the rulings (this session's reading, not a ruling):** the month name and the weekday row take ONE
  style each for the whole calendar (they repeat on every page). Under "only sizes that fit" they offer their current
  size or smaller (17 px, 11 px): they set the height of the band above the weeks, and the PDF divides what is left
  between the weeks (`mvlFrame`). Both 12 px caps are arithmetic and must be measured before the size lists are fixed.
- Rough size: L, 2–3 sessions (plan §9).

  uncommitted entry.

#### The scoping answer that came first (kept for its numbers)
- **Where the size lives today:** screen `.mv-bars{grid-auto-rows:17px}` (fixed lanes; a bar stretches to 17 px) and
  `.mv-bar{10px 600; line-height:1.5; padding:1px 8px}`. In print, lanes are `minmax(17px, auto)` and bars are
  `align-self:start`, so 19 px. The writer copies both (`MVL_PAINT.bar`, `laneMin`), and `mvlLaneTracks` already
  grows a lane to its tallest one-lane bar. `mvPillLineCount` / `mvlPillLines` (one-day custom pills that wrap)
  measure at 10 px.
- **Recommended route (not ruled):** an opt-in setting. When it is on, a body class and rules scoped to `#table-wrap`
  enlarge `.mv-pill` and make the screen's lanes content-sized like print's, so only lanes holding a bar grow. The
  writer takes a pill scale in `mvlWeekPaint`. Off = byte-identical (gates 10 and 13). It is still a change to the frozen surface's
  look, so it needs the owner's specific instruction plus the gates. Open questions: what grows, global or per
  phase, which sizes, file or preference, and whether the print rollback follows (it's the monthemit oracle for the writer).
- ⚠️ Dense months already shrink in the PDF (`fitMonthLayout`'s k), which would undo bigger bars where they matter
  most; measure on the fixtures before choosing sizes. The calendar zoom already enlarges the screen month view.

### 📝 27 Oct 2026: MAKE THE LIVE WATERFALL LOOK LIKE ITS PDF — PLANNED, rulings R0–R7 OPEN, no app code ([`WATERFALL-PDF-LOOK-PLAN.md`](WATERFALL-PDF-LOOK-PLAN.md))

  export as closely as possible … the canvas can be flexibile as it currently is … while maintaining all
  functionality and ease of use"*. Tracker card `waterfall-pdf-look`, planned.

  columns, body type, fills and text widths already match exactly (a note: 129.11 px on screen, 116.20 pt at the
  PDF's 0.90 fit). What differs: rows 20 px vs 15; lines (always-on `#D4D4D4` grid and a 2 px frame vs the
  preference's lines, a 1.2 frame and year separators); the heading row (`#F2F2F2`, 11 px, dividers vs `#D9D9D9`,
  8.25 bold, none); the title block (a 70.5 px bar, 13/11 px vs no bar, 11/8); the "+" in every empty Notes cell.
  ⚠️ A three-line PDF header is always squeezed to fit the top margin: measured ×0.742 (title 8.2 pt, lines 5.9 pt).

  `body.wf-look-pdf #table-wrap` (never reaches `#print-root`); four small frozen edits for the row geometry
  (L1–L4, plus L5 only if measurement calls for it), all skipped under `wfPrintRender`; rules painted on cell edges
  so they take no room; row drags seeded from the STORED height (closes MANTINE-SEAM §5.7). Exports byte-identical
  throughout, no save-format change. Step 0 is a `wflook` leg that compares the screen to the PDF's drawing operators.

  (gridlines on screen: reverses the 3 Sep ruling), and R3–R7. Each has a recommended default.

- **Step 4 committed** as `33d0a98`. **Step 5 DONE, uncommitted:** Help (new "Getting around the Month view"; the
  export line no longer claims screen = PDF), `gate.sh` (monthmap ×3, softlines ×2), UI-CONVENTIONS §7 + §10 gate 20,
  MANTINE-SEAM §4.2, CLAUDE.md's freeze section, README Unreleased entry (plan §16).
- **Step 6, the full gate (plan §17), found two real bugs and one test made honest.** Every frozen output was
  byte-identical in both gates so far (waterfall PDF, Excel, gates 10 and 13).
  - Gate 1 (3,132 / 4): the map appeared a task AFTER a switch to the month view, and the calendar dropped ~70 px under
    what was placed against it (`wfmenu` V1/V2, `weeknum`). Fixed: `update()` pushes the map before `render()`, and
    MonthMap commits that push synchronously. The calendar now moves 0 px on a switch.
  - Gate 2 (3,132 / 3): ⚠️ **`flushSync` on every push deferred OTHER panels' updates** (`notescols` A9: Settings'
    Hidden Notes row stale at the check, right a second later). Now it runs only on a push that changes the map's
    HEIGHT (shows / rows / month count), so the waterfall never calls it. `notescols` 49/49 ×2.
  - Gate 2's `halfworked` EX was NOT a flake (base 10/10, branch 5/10): the map makes `#table-wrap`, the month's scroll
    box, ~69 px shorter, so the leg's scroll is real, and its event lands late under virtual time and closes the
    month menu (any scroll closes it). Fixed in the leg only (mvmenu's scroll-when-needed, plus one re-click on
    evidence of a late scroll); no assertion changed; 9/9 + 3/3, base 5/5. Waiting for the event and poking a frame
    were tried first and do not work; the plan records both.
  - `pilllabel (shootorder)` X2 "months 1 vs 18" is the contention flake already on record; passed 2/2 alone.
  - ✅ **Gate 3: 3,142 PASS, 0 FAIL, GATE PASSED** (`tests/harness/gate-step6c.log` in the worktree). Ten more
    checks than gate 2, matched leg by leg: its 3 failures pass, and `halfworked` runs 24 checks, not 17.
  - Switch timed in the visible pane (1440 × 900, `mvnotes`): month → waterfall 108–127 ms, waterfall → month
    15–26 ms. Same as step 2 and `ae39e3e`'s ~110 ms.
  - ⚠️ **Before the merge:** the branch forked at `fe9c2f6c` and lacks main's `31eddd0` (switch ~4× faster, frozen edit
    L1) and `273692c` (phase start/end notes). A dry-run merge of the committed steps is clean. After merging,
    re-time month → waterfall (the map's synchronous unmount is a bigger share of a ~25 ms switch), then a full gate.
  - **Steps 5–6 committed as `819e11a`** (owner's picker; the three `gate-step6*.log` files stay untracked in the
    worktree). Owner chose a **fresh session for the merge**: merge main in, re-time the switch, full gate, then ask
    (picker) about merging into local `main`, and separately about push. Nothing has reached `main` yet.
  - What a user sees: a dense month scrolls ~69 px sooner (the map's planned height); the month menu's "any scroll
    closes it" rule is met a bit more often (unchanged; `month-menu-trim`, ready on its own branch, narrows it to
    calendar scrolls, which this still is).

- **Asked** (owner): *"Offer me 5 options for redesigns of the look of the month view calendar page. I want it to look
  a little more modern. I also dont want the buttons to change the months to be on the page itself, they should be
  fixed buttons above"*. Tracker cards `month-view-modern-look` and `month-nav-in-toolbar`, both requested.
- **The mockup:** one synthetic October 2026, six tabs (today's look, copied from `legacy.css`, plus five themes laid
  over the SAME markup `renderMonthView` emits): 1 Soft Lines (‹ month › in the toolbar after the view switch),
  2 Clean Sheet (fixed row: month name left, Today + ‹ › right), 3 Month Rail (a strip of every month with phase
  dots), 4 Navy Band (‹ month ▾ › at the toolbar's right end, the month opens a list), 5 Tiles (centred pill). Looks
  and button placements are independent, so the owner may mix them. Not committed.
- **Then** (owner): *"Lets see a mockup of version 1 with the calendar left right selector on the right side of the
  screen in-line with the styling menu bar"*. Added as tab **1b**: Option 1's look, toolbar untouched, and
  `‹ October 2026 ›` at the right end of the text style bar's row (the bar gives up the width; the selector sits
  outside the bar's box, its right edge on the undo/redo buttons' above). Owner reviewing.
- **Then** (owner): *"Mockup a few variations of the month rail"*. Tabs **3a-3d**, all on Option 1's look: 3a Timeline
  (every month, thin phase lines under them, ~58 px row), 3b Compact (seven months at the right of the style bar's
  row, no extra row), 3c By year (one joined bar per year, phase dots, ~60 px), 3d Phase map (named phase bars over
  the months; a phase click jumps to its first month, ~100 px). The mockup's month controls now respond (the calendar
  stays on October); its rails are drawn from per-phase date spans, packed into shared tracks as the waterfall packs
  columns. A real rail would read `monthRangeForSchedule()` and the schedule's phase segments.
- **Then** (owner): *"plan 3d"*, and mid-plan *"maybe the position of the phase map rail should be swapped with the
  position of the styling bar"*: tab **3d+** (toolbar → phase map → style bar → calendar). Not planned yet; my honest
  concerns went to the owner first: the map's height (~100 px, about one week row) grows with every overlapping
  phase (the mockup's made-up show needs only 3 rows), a long show squeezes or scrolls it, and soft grey lines on
  screen with black lines in the PDF break what-you-see-is-what-prints. Proposed guardrails: rows ~14 px, at most 3
  named rows (more as thin lines), measured on real fixtures first.
- **Build facts gathered for the plan:** the rail must be a third line INSIDE `.view-toggle-row` (PreviewToolbar.jsx
  renders the style bar's host there; a sibling between that row and `#table-wrap` breaks the contract). React from a
  new bridge key, clicks back by delegated data attributes (the `hiddenNotes` pattern). The month range is
  `monthRangeForSchedule()` (core ±1 month); the cursor is `monthCursor`, clamped in `update()` before `render()`.
  Phases are `schedule.segments` (`color`, `textColor`, `start`, `end`), plus Sim Post from `weeks[].simPost` and the
  all-phase hiatuses. In the month view `#table-wrap` is the flex:1 scroll box, so a taller toolbar row shrinks it
  without touching the grid fit. ~100 harness files click `#mv-prev`/`#mv-next` by script; hidden-not-deleted keeps
  them working.
- ⛔ **What a build must respect.** `.mv-*` CSS and `renderMonthView` are frozen (the print-path month PDF prints
  them; the direct writer parses the HTML and hand-copies the print rules in `MVL_PAINT`). So:
  - Every new rule scoped to `#table-wrap` (never matches inside `#print-root`); gates 10 and 13 byte-identical is the
    acceptance gate. Options 1-4 are paint only (no cell, lane or note changes size); 5 narrows each day ~6 px on
    screen, so notes could wrap differently from the PDF.
  - The arrows `#mv-prev` / `#mv-next` are hidden on screen, never deleted, and the new buttons drive them: the
    shape `#mv-hdr-mode-btn` took to the toolbar (30 Sep 2026). A jump to any month (options 3, 4) needs a small
    new engine setter for `monthCursor`, clamped like the arrow handler's range check.
  - Options 2, 4, 5's "today" marker: the renderer marks no date on a cell, so it would be added from outside after
    each render (sanctioned pattern 3), not by a frozen edit.
- **Open with the owner:** which look, which buttons, and whether the month PDF should follow the new look later
  (that would change `MVL_PAINT` and the print rollback's frozen print rules, and re-cut gate 13 with sign-off).

  scenario). Each has a recommended default.
